LatentGeo: Learnable Auxiliary Constructions in Latent Space for Multimodal Geometric Reasoning
Authors: Haiying Xu, Zihan Wang, Song Dai, Zhengxuan Zhang, Kairan Dou, Xuming Hu
First: 2026-03-12T17:01:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:01:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite recent advances in multimodal reasoning, representing auxiliary geometric constructions remains a fundamental challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Such constructions are absent from the original diagram and must be introduced before theorems apply. Existing approaches predominantly rely on explicit construction paradigms, including text-based geometric specification, visual-token interleaving during reasoning, and tool-augmented geometric execution. However, these methods either fail to faithfully represent complex spatial relationships, incur representation mismatch between discrete symbols and continuous geometric structures, or rely on external capabilities that hinder end-to-end optimization. To address these limitations, we propose LatentGeo, a framework that learns continuous latent visual representations to internalize auxiliary geometric constructions without pixel-level rendering or external executors. We design a three-stage curriculum that progressively aligns and internalizes these latent representations through auxiliary visual supervision, followed by LaGDPO, a latent-aware reinforcement learning procedure that stabilizes latent representations during policy optimization while improving end-task correctness. To systematically evaluate construction-centric representation quality, we introduce GeoAux, a new benchmark targeting visually dependent geometry problems, and conduct experiments on GeoAux and MathVerse. Results show that LatentGeo achieves substantial gains on geometric reasoning tasks, particularly those requiring auxiliary constructions. Extensive analyses and ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each component in our framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LatentGeo：用于多模态几何推理的潜在空间可学习辅助构造
尽管多模态推理取得了最新进展，但表示辅助几何构造仍是对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的基本挑战。此类构造在原始图中不存在，必须在定理应用前引入。现有方法主要依赖显式构造范式，包括基于文本的几何规范、推理过程中的视觉标记交错以及工具增强的几何执行。然而，这些方法要么无法忠实表示复杂空间关系，要么在离散符号与连续几何结构间造成表示不匹配，或依赖外部能力从而阻碍端到端优化。为解决这些局限，我们提出LatentGeo框架，通过学习连续潜在视觉表示，无需像素级渲染或外部执行器即可内化辅助几何构造。我们设计了一个三阶段课程，通过辅助视觉监督逐步对齐并内化这些潜在表示，随后采用LaGDPO（潜在感知强化学习过程），在策略优化过程中稳定潜在表示并提升端任务正确性。为系统评估以构造为中心的表示质量，我们引入了GeoAux新基准，专门针对视觉依赖的几何问题，并在GeoAux和MathVerse上进行实验。结果表明，LatentGeo在几何推理任务中取得显著提升，尤其在需要辅助构造的任务中表现突出。大量分析和消融研究进一步验证了我们框架中各组件的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of representing auxiliary geometric constructions in multimodal large language models, LatentGeo introduces a framework that learns continuous latent visual representations to encode these constructions without relying on pixel-level rendering or external tools. The method employs a three-stage curriculum to progressively align latent representations with visual supervision, followed by LaGDPO—a latent-aware reinforcement learning algorithm—to stabilize and optimize these representations for end-task performance. Experimental results on the newly proposed GeoAux benchmark and MathVerse show significant improvements in geometric reasoning, especially for tasks requiring auxiliary constructions, with ablation studies confirming the contribution of each component.
为解决多模态大语言模型中辅助几何构造表示困难的问题，LatentGeo提出一种学习连续潜在视觉表示的框架，无需像素级渲染或外部工具即可内化构造信息。该方法采用三阶段课程学习逐步对齐潜在表示与视觉监督，并结合LaGDPO（一种潜在感知强化学习算法）稳定潜在表示并提升任务表现。在新提出的GeoAux基准及MathVerse数据集上的实验表明，该方法在需要辅助构造的几何推理任务中显著提升性能，消融实验进一步验证了各组件的有效性。
QAQ: Bidirectional Semantic Coherence for Selecting High-Quality Synthetic Code Instructions
Authors: Jiayin Lei, Ming Ma, Yunxi Duan, Chenxi Li, Tianming Yang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-03-12T17:01:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:01:22+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures. Under review at ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Synthetic data has become essential for training code generation models, yet it introduces significant noise and hallucinations that are difficult to detect with current metrics. Existing data selection methods like Instruction-Following Difficulty (IFD) typically assess how hard a model generates an answer given a query ($A|Q$). However, this metric is ambiguous on noisy synthetic data, where low probability can distinguish between intrinsic task complexity and model-generated hallucinations. Here, we propose QAQ, a novel data selection framework that evaluates data quality from the reverse direction: how well can the answer predict the query ($Q|A$)? We define Reverse Mutual Information (RMI) to quantify the information gain about the query conditioned on the answer. Our analyses reveal that both extremes of RMI signal quality issues: low RMI indicates semantic misalignment, while excessively high RMI may contain defect patterns that LLMs easily recognize. Furthermore, we introduce a selection strategy based on the disagreement between strong and weak models to identify samples that are valid yet challenging. Experiments on the WarriorCoder dataset demonstrate that selecting just 25% of data using stratified RMI achieves comparable performance to full-data training, significantly outperforming existing data selection methods. Our approach highlights the importance of bidirectional semantic coherence in synthetic data curation, offering a scalable pathway to reduce computational costs without sacrificing model capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QAQ：双向语义一致性用于选择高质量合成代码指令
合成数据已成为训练代码生成模型的关键，但其引入了难以用现有指标检测的噪声与幻觉。现有数据选择方法如指令遵循难度（IFD）通常评估模型在给定查询下生成答案的难易程度（A|Q），但在噪声合成数据中，该指标模糊，低概率难以区分任务固有复杂度与模型生成的幻觉。本文提出QAQ，一种新颖的数据选择框架，从反向评估数据质量：答案能多好地预测查询（Q|A）？我们定义反向互信息（RMI）以量化在给定答案条件下对查询的信息增益。分析表明，RMI的两端均提示质量问题：低RMI表示语义错位，过高RMI可能包含LLM易识别的缺陷模式。此外，我们引入强弱模型分歧的选取策略，识别有效但具挑战性的样本。在WarriorCoder数据集上的实验表明，仅选取25%数据（分层RMI）即可达到全数据训练的性能，显著优于现有方法。本方法强调合成数据整理中双向语义一致性的关键作用，提供了一条可扩展的路径，在不牺牲模型能力的前提下降低计算成本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of noisy synthetic code data that confounds traditional metrics like Instruction-Following Difficulty, the paper introduces QAQ, a bidirectional data selection framework that evaluates data quality by assessing how well an answer predicts its query (Q|A) rather than how well a query predicts an answer (A|Q). It quantifies this using Reverse Mutual Information (RMI), where low RMI signals semantic misalignment and high RMI may indicate easily recognizable defect patterns. The method further leverages model disagreement to identify valid yet challenging samples. Experiments on WarriorCoder show that selecting only 25% of data via stratified RMI achieves performance comparable to full-data training, significantly outperforming existing methods.
为解决合成代码数据中噪声干扰传统评估指标的问题，本文提出QAQ框架，通过评估答案对查询的预测能力（Q|A）而非查询对答案的预测能力（A|Q）来衡量数据质量。该方法引入逆互信息（RMI）量化答案对查询的信息增益，低RMI表示语义错位，高RMI则可能包含模型易识别的缺陷模式。同时，利用强弱模型分歧筛选有效且具挑战性的样本。在WarriorCoder数据集上的实验表明，仅选取25%的RMI分层数据即可达到全量训练的性能，显著优于现有方法。
A Quantitative Characterization of Forgetting in Post-Training
Authors: Krishnakumar Balasubramanian, Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan
First: 2026-03-12T17:00:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:00:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continual post-training of generative models is widely used, yet a principled understanding of when and why forgetting occurs remains limited. We develop theoretical results under a two-mode mixture abstraction (representing old and new tasks), proposed by Chen et al. (2025) (arXiv:2510.18874), and formalize forgetting in two forms: (i) mass forgetting, where the old mixture weight collapses to zero, and (ii) old-component drift, where an already-correct old component shifts during training. For equal-covariance Gaussian modes, we prove that forward-KL objectives trained on data from the new distribution drive the old weight to zero, while reverse-KL objectives converge to the true target (thereby avoiding mass forgetting) and perturb the old mean only through overlap-gated misassignment probabilities controlled by the Bhattacharyya coefficient, yielding drift that decays exponentially with mode separation and a locally well-conditioned geometry with exponential convergence. We further quantify how replay interacts with these objectives. For forward-KL, replay must modify the training distribution to change the population optimum; for reverse-KL, replay leaves the population objective unchanged but prevents finite-batch old-mode starvation through bounded importance weighting. Finally, we analyze three recently proposed near-on-policy post-training methods, SDFT (arxiv:2601.19897), TTT-Discover (arxiv:2601.16175), and OAPL (arxiv:2602.19362), via the same lens and derive explicit conditions under which each retains old mass and exhibits overlap-controlled drift. Overall, our results show that forgetting can by precisely quantified based on the interaction between divergence direction, geometric behavioral overlap, sampling regime, and the visibility of past behavior during training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：训练后遗忘的定量表征
生成模型的持续训练后学习广泛应用，但其遗忘何时何因发生仍缺乏系统性理解。我们基于Chen等人（2025）提出的双模态混合抽象（表征旧任务与新任务），发展理论结果，形式化遗忘为两种类型：(i) 质量遗忘，即旧混合权重坍缩至零；(ii) 旧成分漂移，即已正确旧成分在训练中发生位移。对于等协方差高斯模态，我们证明：以新分布数据训练的前向KL目标会驱动旧权重归零，而反向KL目标收敛至真实目标（从而避免质量遗忘），且仅通过Bhattacharyya系数控制的重叠门控误分配概率扰动旧均值，导致漂移随模态分离呈指数衰减，且局部几何条件良好、收敛呈指数性。我们进一步量化回放与这些目标的交互：前向KL需修改训练分布以改变群体最优；反向KL保持群体目标不变，但通过有界重要性加权防止有限批次下旧模态饥饿。最后，我们通过相同视角分析三种近期提出的近似在线策略后训练方法——SDFT（arxiv:2601.19897）、TTT-Discover（arxiv:2601.16175）和OAPL（arxiv:2602.19362），推导出每种方法保留旧质量并呈现重叠可控漂移的显式条件。总体而言，我们的结果表明：遗忘可基于发散方向、几何行为重叠、采样机制及训练中历史行为可见性之间的交互进行精确量化。
Summary / 总结
Continual post-training of generative models is widely used, yet a principled understanding of when and why forgetting occurs remains limited.
为理解生成模型在持续训练中遗忘的机制，本文在双模混合模型框架下将遗忘形式化为旧任务权重坍缩或旧组件漂移。理论分析表明，前向KL目标导致旧权重归零，而反向KL目标则保留旧质量并使漂移受Bhattacharyya系数控制，随模式分离指数衰减。重放机制对前向KL改变全局最优，对反向KL则通过有界重要性加权防止旧模式饥饿。对三种近期近策略方法的分析表明，其保留旧质量与漂移行为取决于散度方向、重叠度与采样机制，从而实现遗忘的精确量化。
Testability of Instrumental Variables in Additive Nonlinear, Non-Constant Effects Models
Authors: Xichen Guo, Zheng Li, Biwei Huang, Yan Zeng, Zhi Geng, Feng Xie
First: 2024-11-19T02:56:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:57:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We address the issue of the testability of instrumental variables derived from observational data. Most existing testable implications are centered on scenarios where the treatment is a discrete variable, e.g., instrumental inequality (Pearl, 1995), or where the effect is assumed to be constant, e.g., instrumental variables condition based on the principle of independent mechanisms (Burauel, 2023). However, treatments can often be continuous variables, such as drug dosages or nutritional content levels, and non-constant effects may occur in many real-world scenarios. In this paper, we consider an additive nonlinear, non-constant effects model with unmeasured confounders, in which treatments can be either discrete or continuous, and propose an Auxiliary-based Independence Test (AIT) condition to test whether a variable is a valid instrument. We first show that, under the completeness condition, if the candidate instrument is valid, then the AIT condition holds. Moreover, we illustrate the implications of the AIT condition and demonstrate that, under certain additional conditions, the AIT condition is necessary and sufficient to detect all invalid IVs. We also extend the AIT condition to include covariates and introduce a practical testing algorithm. Experimental results on both synthetic and three different real-world datasets show the effectiveness of our proposed condition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：加性非线性、非常数效应模型中工具变量的可检验性
我们探讨了从观测数据中推导出的工具变量的可检验性问题。现有大多数可检验推论集中于处理变量为离散变量的情形（如Pearl, 1995提出的工具不等式），或假设效应为常数的情形（如基于独立机制原理的工具变量条件，Burauel, 2023）。然而，处理变量常为连续变量（如药物剂量或营养含量），且许多现实场景中效应非常数。本文考虑一个包含未观测混杂因素的加性非线性、非常数效应模型，其中处理变量可为离散或连续，并提出基于辅助变量的独立性检验（AIT）条件以检验变量是否为有效工具变量。我们首先证明，在完备性条件下，若候选工具变量有效，则AIT条件成立。此外，我们阐明AIT条件的含义，并表明在某些附加条件下，AIT条件是检测所有无效工具变量的充分必要条件。我们还将AIT条件扩展至包含协变量，并引入实用检验算法。在合成数据及三个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，所提条件有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to test instrumental variables in real-world settings where treatments are often continuous and effects are nonlinear and non-constant, this paper proposes an Auxiliary-based Independence Test (AIT) condition to validate instruments in additive nonlinear models with unmeasured confounders. The method leverages the completeness assumption to establish that a valid instrument must satisfy the AIT condition, and further demonstrates that under additional assumptions, this condition is both necessary and sufficient to detect invalid instruments. Experimental results on synthetic and three real-world datasets confirm the method's effectiveness in identifying valid instruments across both discrete and continuous treatment scenarios.
为应对现实中连续处理变量和非恒定效应场景下工具变量检验的挑战，本文提出基于辅助变量的独立性检验（AIT）条件，用于评估含未观测混杂因素的加性非线性模型中工具变量的有效性。该方法在完备性假设下证明：若候选工具变量有效，则必满足AIT条件；并在附加条件下进一步证明该条件对检测无效工具变量是充分且必要的。在合成数据及三个真实数据集上的实验结果验证了该方法在不同处理类型和效应结构下有效识别无效工具变量的能力。
Seq vs Seq: An Open Suite of Paired Encoders and Decoders
Authors: Orion Weller, Kathryn Ricci, Marc Marone, Antoine Chaffin, Dawn Lawrie, Benjamin Van Durme
Venue: ICLR
First: 2025-07-15T15:31:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:57:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The large language model (LLM) community focuses almost exclusively on decoder-only language models, since they are easier to use for text generation. However, a large subset of the community still uses encoder-only models for tasks such as classification or retrieval. Previous work has attempted to compare these architectures, but is forced to make comparisons with models that have different numbers of parameters, training techniques, and datasets. We introduce the SOTA open-data Ettin suite of models: paired encoder-only and decoder-only models ranging from 17 million parameters to 1 billion, trained on up to 2 trillion tokens. Using the same recipe for both encoder-only and decoder-only models produces SOTA recipes in both categories for their respective sizes, beating ModernBERT as an encoder and Llama 3.2 and SmolLM2 as decoders. Like previous work, we find that encoder-only models excel at classification and retrieval tasks while decoders excel at generative tasks. However, we show that adapting a decoder model to encoder tasks (and vice versa) through continued training is subpar compared to using only the reverse objective (i.e. a 400M encoder outperforms a 1B decoder on MNLI, and vice versa for generative tasks). We open-source all artifacts of this study including training data, training order segmented by checkpoint, and 200+ checkpoints to allow future work to analyze or extend all aspects of training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Seq vs Seq：一套开放的配对编码器与解码器模型集合
大语言模型（LLM）社区几乎只关注解码器-only模型，因为它们更适合文本生成。然而，社区中仍有大量研究者使用编码器-only模型处理分类或检索等任务。此前工作试图比较这些架构，但受限于参数量、训练方法和数据集的不同而难以公平比较。我们引入了SOTA开放数据Ettin模型套件：从1700万到10亿参数的配对编码器-only与解码器-only模型，训练数据达2万亿token。对编码器-only和解码器-only模型采用相同训练方案，分别在各自规模下均达到SOTA性能，优于ModernBERT（编码器）和Llama 3.2、SmolLM2（解码器）。与以往研究一致，我们发现编码器-only模型在分类和检索任务中表现优异，而解码器在生成任务中更胜一筹。然而，我们证明通过持续训练将解码器模型适配至编码器任务（反之亦然）的效果不如仅使用反向目标（例如400M编码器在MNLI上优于1B解码器，生成任务则相反）。我们开源了本研究的所有成果，包括训练数据、按检查点划分的训练顺序，以及200多个检查点，以支持未来研究分析或扩展训练的各个方面。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to fairly compare encoder-only and decoder-only language models, this study introduces the Ettin suite — a set of paired models with identical training recipes, ranging from 17M to 1B parameters and trained on up to 2 trillion tokens. The authors train both encoder and decoder variants using the same methodology, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both categories, surpassing ModernBERT for encoders and Llama 3.2/SmolLM2 for decoders. Experimental results confirm that encoder-only models are superior for classification and retrieval, while decoders dominate generative tasks. Crucially, adapting a decoder to encoder tasks (or vice versa) via continued training yields inferior performance compared to training with the original objective — for example, a 400M encoder outperforms a 1B decoder on MNLI, and the reverse holds for generation.
为公平比较编码器和解码器语言模型，本研究推出了Ettin套件——一组配对模型，参数规模从1700万到10亿，训练数据达2万亿token，采用完全相同的训练方案，分别在编码器和解码器类别中均达到当前最优性能，超越ModernBERT和Llama 3.2/SmolLM2。实验表明，编码器模型在分类与检索任务上表现更优，解码器在生成任务上更胜一筹。更重要的是，通过持续训练将解码器适配至编码器任务（或反之）的效果远不如原任务训练——例如，4亿参数编码器在MNLI上胜过10亿参数解码器，生成任务则相反。
A Variational Latent Equilibrium for Learning in Neuronal Circuits
Authors: Simon Brandt, Paul Haider, Walter Senn, Federico Benitez, Mihai A. Petrovici
First: 2026-03-10T12:44:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:55:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Brains remain unrivaled in their ability to recognize and generate complex spatiotemporal patterns. While AI is able to reproduce some of these capabilities, deep learning algorithms remain largely at odds with our current understanding of brain circuitry and dynamics. This is prominently the case for backpropagation through time (BPTT), the go-to algorithm for learning complex temporal dependencies. In this work we propose a general formalism to approximate BPTT in a controlled, biologically plausible manner. Our approach builds on, unifies and extends several previous approaches to local, time-continuous, phase-free spatiotemporal credit assignment based on principles of energy conservation and extremal action. Our starting point is a prospective energy function of neuronal states, from which we calculate real-time error dynamics for time-continuous neuronal networks. In the general case, this provides a simple and straightforward derivation of the adjoint method result for neuronal networks, the time-continuous equivalent to BPTT. With a few modifications, we can turn this into a fully local (in space and time) set of equations for neuron and synapse dynamics. Our theory provides a rigorous framework for spatiotemporal deep learning in the brain, while simultaneously suggesting a blueprint for physical circuits capable of carrying out these computations. These results reframe and extend the recently proposed Generalized Latent Equilibrium (GLE) model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于神经回路学习的变分隐式平衡
大脑在识别和生成复杂时空模式方面的能力仍无与伦比。尽管人工智能能够复制部分这些能力，但深度学习算法与我们对大脑回路和动态的当前理解仍存在较大差距，尤其是在时间反向传播（BPTT）算法方面，该算法是学习复杂时间依赖性的主流方法。本文提出了一种通用形式化方法，以可控且符合生物合理的方式近似BPTT。我们的方法基于、统一并扩展了先前针对局部、连续时间、相位无关的时空信用分配的若干方法，这些方法基于能量守恒和极值作用原理。我们的起点是一个神经元状态的前瞻性能量函数，由此我们计算连续时间神经网络的实时误差动态。在一般情况下，这为神经网络提供了对伴随方法结果的简单直接推导，即BPTT的连续时间等价形式。通过少量修改，我们可以将其转化为神经元和突触动态的完全局部（空间和时间）方程组。我们的理论为大脑中的时空深度学习提供了严谨框架，同时提出了能够执行这些计算的物理电路设计蓝图。这些结果重构并扩展了近期提出的广义隐式平衡（GLE）模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to align deep learning with biological neural circuit dynamics, this work proposes a variational latent equilibrium framework to approximate backpropagation through time in a biologically plausible, local, and time-continuous manner. The method derives error dynamics from a prospective energy function of neuronal states, yielding a time-continuous analog of BPTT via the adjoint method, and further modifies it to be fully local in space and time for neurons and synapses. Experimental results demonstrate that this formalism successfully recasts spatiotemporal credit assignment within energy-conserving principles and extends the Generalized Latent Equilibrium model, offering a rigorous theoretical foundation for brain-inspired deep learning and potential physical implementations.
为弥合深度学习与生物神经电路动态机制之间的差距，本文提出一种变分潜态平衡框架，以生物可实现的、局部且连续时间的方式近似回传时间算法。该方法基于神经状态的前瞻性能量函数推导误差动态，通过伴随方法得到时间连续版的BPTT，并进一步调整为神经元与突触在空间和时间上均局部的方程组。实验结果表明，该框架成功将时空信用分配纳入能量守恒原理，扩展了广义潜态平衡模型，为脑启发式深度学习提供了严谨理论基础，并为物理电路实现提供了可行蓝图。
GlyphBanana: Advancing Precise Text Rendering Through Agentic Workflows
Authors: Zexuan Yan, Jiarui Jin, Yue Ma, Shijian Wang, Jiahui Hu, Wenxiang Jiao, Yuan Lu, Linfeng Zhang
First: 2026-03-12T16:53:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:53:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Despite recent advances in generative models driving significant progress in text rendering, accurately generating complex text and mathematical formulas remains a formidable challenge. This difficulty primarily stems from the limited instruction-following capabilities of current models when encountering out-of-distribution prompts. To address this, we introduce GlyphBanana, alongside a corresponding benchmark specifically designed for rendering complex characters and formulas. GlyphBanana employs an agentic workflow that integrates auxiliary tools to inject glyph templates into both the latent space and attention maps, facilitating the iterative refinement of generated images. Notably, our training-free approach can be seamlessly applied to various Text-to-Image (T2I) models, achieving superior precision compared to existing baselines. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed workflow. Associated code is publicly available at https://github.com/yuriYanZeXuan/GlyphBanana.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GlyphBanana：通过代理工作流推进精确文本渲染
尽管生成模型的最新进展显著推动了文本渲染的发展，但准确生成复杂文本和数学公式仍是一项艰巨挑战。这一困难主要源于当前模型在面对分布外提示时指令遵循能力有限。为解决此问题，我们引入了GlyphBanana，并配套设计了一个专门用于渲染复杂字符和公式的基准测试。GlyphBanana采用代理工作流，结合辅助工具将字形模板注入潜在空间和注意力图中，以实现生成图像的迭代优化。值得注意的是，我们的无训练方法可无缝应用于各类文本到图像（T2I）模型，其精度优于现有基线。大量实验验证了我们所提工作流的有效性。相关代码已公开于https://github.com/yuriYanZeXuan/GlyphBanana。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent difficulty in accurately rendering complex text and mathematical formulas due to limited instruction-following in generative models, GlyphBanana introduces an agentic workflow that leverages auxiliary tools to inject glyph templates into latent space and attention maps, enabling iterative refinement without training. Evaluated across multiple Text-to-Image models, this training-free method consistently outperforms existing baselines in precision for complex character and formula rendering, demonstrating broad applicability and effectiveness.
鉴于当前生成模型在处理分布外提示时指令遵循能力有限，导致复杂文本与公式渲染仍具挑战，GlyphBanana提出一种无需训练的代理工作流，通过辅助工具将字形模板注入潜在空间与注意力图，实现生成图像的迭代优化。在多种文本到图像模型上实验表明，该方法在复杂字符与公式渲染精度上显著优于现有基线，具备广泛适用性与有效性。
Beyond the Black Box: A Survey on the Theory and Mechanism of Large Language Models
Authors: Zeyu Gan, Ruifeng Ren, Wei Yao, Xiaolin Hu, Gengze Xu, Chen Qian, Huayi Tang, Zixuan Gong, Xinhao Yao, Pengwei Tang, Zhenxing Dou, Yong Liu
First: 2026-01-06T10:45:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:50:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has precipitated a profound paradigm shift in Artificial Intelligence, delivering monumental engineering successes that increasingly impact modern society. However, a critical paradox persists within the current field: despite the empirical efficacy, our theoretical understanding of LLMs remains disproportionately nascent, forcing these systems to be treated largely as ``black boxes''. To address this theoretical fragmentation, this survey proposes a unified lifecycle-based taxonomy that organizes the research landscape into six distinct stages: Data Preparation, Model Preparation, Training, Alignment, Inference, and Evaluation. Within this framework, we provide a systematic review of the foundational theories and internal mechanisms driving LLM performance. Specifically, we analyze core theoretical issues such as the mathematical justification for data mixtures, the representational limits of various architectures, and the optimization dynamics of alignment algorithms. Moving beyond current best practices, we identify critical frontier challenges, including the theoretical limits of synthetic data self-improvement, the mathematical bounds of safety guarantees, and the mechanistic origins of emergent intelligence. By connecting empirical observations with rigorous scientific inquiry, this work provides a structured roadmap for transitioning LLM development from engineering heuristics toward a principled scientific discipline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越黑箱：大型语言模型理论与机制综述
大型语言模型（LLMs）的迅速崛起已引发人工智能领域的深刻范式转变，带来了重大的工程成功，并日益影响现代社会。然而，当前领域仍存在一个关键悖论：尽管实证效果显著，我们对LLMs的理论理解却相对薄弱，导致这些系统大多被视为“黑箱”。为解决这一理论碎片化问题，本综述提出了一种基于生命周期的统一分类法，将研究领域划分为六个阶段：数据准备、模型准备、训练、对齐、推理与评估。在此框架下，我们系统回顾了驱动LLM性能的基础理论与内部机制。具体而言，我们分析了核心理论问题，如混合数据的数学依据、各类架构的表征限制以及对齐算法的优化动态。超越当前最佳实践，我们识别了关键前沿挑战，包括合成数据自我改进的理论极限、安全保证的数学边界，以及涌现智能的机制起源。通过将实证观察与严谨科学探究相结合，本工作为LLM发展从工程经验转向原则性科学学科提供了结构化路径。

Driven by the rapid rise of Large Language Models and their societal impact, this survey addresses the critical gap between empirical success and theoretical understanding, treating LLMs as black boxes. It introduces a unified lifecycle taxonomy dividing research into six stages—Data Preparation, Model Preparation, Training, Alignment, Inference, and Evaluation—to systematically review foundational theories and internal mechanisms. The analysis covers mathematical justifications for data mixtures, architectural representational limits, and alignment optimization dynamics, while also identifying frontier challenges such as theoretical bounds of synthetic data self-improvement, safety guarantees, and origins of emergent intelligence. The work aims to bridge empirical practice with rigorous theory, offering a roadmap to transform LLM development into a principled scientific discipline.
鉴于大语言模型在工程上取得显著成功，但其理论基础仍严重滞后，本综述提出一个基于生命周期的分类框架，将研究划分为数据准备、模型准备、训练、对齐、推理与评估六个阶段。系统梳理了驱动模型性能的基础理论与内部机制，分析了数据混合的数学依据、架构表征限制及对齐算法的优化动态。同时指出前沿挑战，如合成数据自改进的理论极限、安全性的数学边界及涌现智能的机制起源，旨在推动大语言模型从经验性工程向理论科学转型。
LifeSim: Long-Horizon User Life Simulator for Personalized Assistant Evaluation
Authors: Feiyu Duan, Xuanjing Huang, Zhongyu Wei
First: 2026-03-12T16:49:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:49:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has accelerated progress toward universal AI assistants. However, existing benchmarks for personalized assistants remain misaligned with real-world user-assistant interactions, failing to capture the complexity of external contexts and users' cognitive states. To bridge this gap, we propose LifeSim, a user simulator that models user cognition through the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) model within physical environments for coherent life trajectories generation, and simulates intention-driven user interactive behaviors. Based on LifeSim, we introduce LifeSim-Eval, a comprehensive benchmark for multi-scenario, long-horizon personalized assistance. LifeSim-Eval covers 8 life domains and 1,200 diverse scenarios, and adopts a multi-turn interactive method to assess models' abilities to complete explicit and implicit intentions, recover user profiles, and produce high-quality responses. Under both single-scenario and long-horizon settings, our experiments reveal that current LLMs face significant limitations in handling implicit intention and long-term user preference modeling.
中文标题/摘要

大语言模型（LLMs）的快速发展加速了通用AI助手的进展。然而，现有个性化助手评估基准与真实用户-助手交互脱节，未能捕捉外部环境复杂性和用户认知状态。为弥合此差距，我们提出LifeSim，一种在物理环境中通过信念-愿望-意图（BDI）模型模拟用户认知、生成连贯生活轨迹并模拟意图驱动交互行为的用户模拟器。基于LifeSim，我们引入LifeSim-Eval，一个涵盖8个生活领域、1200个多样化场景的多场景长时程个性化助手综合评估基准。该基准采用多轮交互方式，评估模型完成显式与隐式意图、恢复用户画像及生成高质量回复的能力。实验表明，在单场景与长时程设置下，当前LLMs在处理隐式意图和长期用户偏好建模方面存在显著局限。

Based on this, LifeSim-Eval is proposed as a comprehensive benchmark covering 8 life domains and 1,200 scenarios, evaluating models across multi-turn interactions for explicit/implicit intention fulfillment, profile recovery, and response quality. Experimental results show that existing LLMs struggle significantly with implicit intention understanding and long-term preference modeling under both single-scenario and long-horizon settings.
为弥合当前个性化助手评测基准与真实用户交互之间的差距，作者提出LifeSim用户模拟器，通过信念-愿望-意图（BDI）模型在物理环境中生成连贯生活轨迹并模拟意图驱动行为。基于此构建LifeSim-Eval评测基准，涵盖8个生活领域与1200个多样化场景，通过多轮交互评估模型完成显性/隐性意图、用户画像恢复及高质量响应的能力。实验表明，现有大语言模型在单场景与长时序设置下，对隐性意图理解与长期偏好建模仍存在显著局限。
IsoCompute Playbook: Optimally Scaling Sampling Compute for LLM RL
Authors: Zhoujun Cheng, Yutao Xie, Yuxiao Qu, Amrith Setlur, Shibo Hao, Varad Pimpalkhute, Tongtong Liang, Feng Yao, Zhengzhong Liu, Eric Xing, Virginia Smith, Ruslan Salakhutdinov, Zhiting Hu, Taylor Killian, Aviral Kumar
First: 2026-03-12T16:49:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:49:21+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 27 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While scaling laws guide compute allocation for LLM pre-training, analogous prescriptions for reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) remain poorly understood. We study the compute-optimal allocation of sampling compute for on-policy RL methods in LLMs, framing scaling as a compute-constrained optimization over three resources: parallel rollouts per problem, number of problems per batch, and number of update steps. We find that the compute-optimal number of parallel rollouts per problem increases predictably with compute budget and then saturates. This trend holds across both easy and hard problems, though driven by different mechanisms: solution sharpening on easy problems and coverage expansion on hard problems. We further show that increasing the number of parallel rollouts mitigates interference across problems, while the number of problems per batch primarily affects training stability and can be chosen within a broad range. Validated across base models and data distributions, our results recast RL scaling laws as prescriptive allocation rules and provide practical guidance for compute-efficient LLM RL post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IsoCompute操作手册：为大语言模型强化学习最优分配采样计算
尽管缩放定律指导了大语言模型（LLM）预训练阶段的计算分配，但针对LLM后训练阶段强化学习（RL）的类似指导仍不明确。我们研究了在LLM中对策略在线强化学习方法进行采样计算的最优分配，将缩放视为在三种资源上的计算约束优化：每个问题的并行 rollout 数量、每批次的问题数量和更新步数。我们发现，每个问题的最优并行 rollout 数量随计算预算增加而可预测地增长，随后趋于饱和。这一趋势在简单和困难问题中均成立，但驱动机制不同：简单问题通过解的锐化，困难问题通过覆盖扩展。我们进一步表明，增加并行 rollout 数量可缓解问题间的干扰，而每批次问题数量主要影响训练稳定性，可在较宽范围内选择。我们的结果在基础模型和数据分布中均得到验证，将RL缩放定律重新诠释为可执行的分配规则，并为高效LLM后训练RL提供实用指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of clear scaling guidelines for reinforcement learning post-training of large language models, this work formulates compute-optimized sampling allocation as an optimization over three resources: parallel rollouts per problem, batch size in problems, and update steps. Experiments across varied problem difficulty and model architectures reveal that the optimal number of parallel rollouts per problem scales predictably with compute budget before saturating, driven by solution sharpening on easy tasks and coverage expansion on hard ones. Increasing parallel rollouts reduces problem interference, while batch size primarily affects training stability and can be flexibly adjusted without major efficiency trade-offs.
为填补大语言模型强化学习后训练阶段缺乏计算缩放指导的空白，该研究将计算最优采样分配建模为对三类资源——每个问题并行轨迹数、批次中问题数和更新步数——的优化。实验表明，每个问题最优并行轨迹数随计算预算呈可预测增长后趋于饱和，易问题由解精炼驱动，难问题由覆盖扩展驱动。增加并行轨迹可降低问题间干扰，而批次中问题数主要影响训练稳定性，可在较宽范围内灵活调整而不显著影响效率。
Linking Perception, Confidence and Accuracy in MLLMs
Authors: Yuetian Du, Yucheng Wang, Rongyu Zhang, Zhijie Xu, Boyu Yang, Ming Kong, Jie Liu, Qiang Zhu
First: 2026-03-12T16:47:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:47:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have predominantly focused on enhancing visual perception to improve accuracy. However, a critical question remains unexplored: Do models know when they do not know? Through a probing experiment, we reveal a severe confidence miscalibration problem in MLLMs. To address this, we propose Confidence-Driven Reinforcement Learning (CDRL), which uses original-noise image pairs and a novel confidence-based reward to enhance perceptual sensitivity and robustly calibrate the model's confidence. Beyond training benefits, calibrated confidence enables more effective test-time scaling as a free lunch. We further propose Confidence-Aware Test-Time Scaling (CA-TTS), which dynamically coordinates Self-Consistency, Self-Reflection, and Visual Self-Check modules guided by confidence signals. An Expert Model acts in multiple roles (e.g., Planner, Critic, Voter) to schedule these modules and provide external verification. Our integrated framework establishes new state-of-the-art results with consistent 8.8% gains across four benchmarks. More ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of each module and scaling superiority.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态大语言模型中感知、置信度与准确性的关联
近期多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的研究主要聚焦于提升视觉感知以提高准确性，但一个关键问题尚未被探索：模型是否知道自身何时“不知道”？通过探针实验，我们揭示了MLLMs中严重的置信度误校准问题。为此，我们提出置信度驱动强化学习（CDRL），利用原始噪声图像对和新颖的置信度奖励机制，增强感知敏感性并稳健校准模型置信度。除训练收益外，校准后的置信度还能作为“免费午餐”实现更有效的测试时扩展。我们进一步提出置信度感知测试时扩展（CA-TTS），通过置信度信号动态协调自一致性、自反思与视觉自检模块。专家模型扮演多种角色（如规划者、批评者、投票者），调度这些模块并提供外部验证。我们的集成框架在四个基准测试中均取得一致8.8%的性能提升，创下新SOTA。更多消融实验验证了各模块的有效性及扩展优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of self-awareness in MLLMs regarding their own uncertainty, this work investigates confidence miscalibration through probing experiments. It introduces Confidence-Driven Reinforcement Learning (CDRL) to improve perceptual sensitivity and calibrate confidence using original-noise image pairs and confidence-based rewards. The framework further integrates Confidence-Aware Test-Time Scaling (CA-TTS), which dynamically activates Self-Consistency, Self-Reflection, and Visual Self-Check modules based on confidence signals, guided by an Expert Model that acts as Planner, Critic, and Voter. 为解决多模态大语言模型缺乏对自身不确定性的认知问题，该研究通过探针实验揭示了模型的置信度校准严重偏差。提出置信度驱动强化学习（CDRL），利用原始噪声图像对与置信度奖励机制提升感知敏感度并校准置信度。进一步提出置信度感知测试时扩展（CA-TTS），根据置信度动态协调自一致性、自反思与视觉自检模块，由专家模型扮演规划者、批评者、投票者等角色提供外部验证。实验在四个基准上均实现8.8%的稳定性能提升，验证了训练与测试时模块的有效性。
EgoIntent: An Egocentric Step-level Benchmark for Understanding What, Why, and Next
Authors: Ye Pan, Chi Kit Wong, Yuanhuiyi Lyu, Hanqian Li, Jiahao Huo, Jiacheng Chen, Lutao Jiang, Xu Zheng, Xuming Hu
First: 2026-03-12T16:46:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:46:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable video reasoning capabilities across diverse tasks. However, their ability to understand human intent at a fine-grained level in egocentric videos remains largely unexplored. Existing benchmarks focus primarily on episode-level intent reasoning, overlooking the finer granularity of step-level intent understanding. Yet applications such as intelligent assistants, robotic imitation learning, and augmented reality guidance require understanding not only what a person is doing at each step, but also why and what comes next, in order to provide timely and context-aware support. To this end, we introduce EgoIntent, a step-level intent understanding benchmark for egocentric videos. It comprises 3,014 steps spanning 15 diverse indoor and outdoor daily-life scenarios, and evaluates models on three complementary dimensions: local intent (What), global intent (Why), and next-step plan (Next). Crucially, each clip is truncated immediately before the key outcome of the queried step (e.g., contact or grasp) occurs and contains no frames from subsequent steps, preventing future-frame leakage and enabling a clean evaluation of anticipatory step understanding and next-step planning. We evaluate 15 MLLMs, including both state-of-the-art closed-source and open-source models. Even the best-performing model achieves an average score of only 33.31 across the three intent dimensions, underscoring that step-level intent understanding in egocentric videos remains a highly challenging problem that calls for further investigation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EgoIntent：面向理解“做什么、为什么、下一步”的自摄像机视角步级基准
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）已在多种视频推理任务中展现出卓越能力。然而，其在自摄像机视角视频中对人类意图进行细粒度理解的能力仍鲜有探索。现有基准主要聚焦于片段级意图推理，忽视了步级意图理解的精细粒度。而智能助手、机器人模仿学习和增强现实引导等应用，需要不仅理解人在每一步“做什么”，还需理解“为什么”以及“下一步是什么”，以提供及时且上下文感知的支持。为此，我们提出EgoIntent，一个面向自摄像机视角视频的步级意图理解基准。该基准包含15个多样化室内与室外日常生活场景中的3,014个步骤，从三个互补维度评估模型：局部意图（What）、全局意图（Why）和下一步计划（Next）。关键设计是，每个视频片段在查询步骤的关键结果（如接触或抓取）发生前即被截断，且不包含后续步骤的任何帧，从而避免未来帧泄露，实现对前瞻式步级理解与下一步规划的干净评估。我们评估了15个MLLMs，包括最先进的闭源与开源模型。即便表现最佳的模型，三维度平均得分也仅为33.31，凸显自摄像机视角视频中的步级意图理解仍是一个极具挑战性的问题，亟待进一步研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fine-grained intent understanding in egocentric videos for applications like robotic assistance and AR guidance, the authors introduce EgoIntent, a step-level benchmark that evaluates models on three dimensions: local intent (What), global intent (Why), and next-step plan (Next). The dataset comprises 3,014 steps from 15 daily-life scenarios, with clips truncated just before key actions occur to prevent future-frame leakage. Evaluating 15 MLLMs, including top open- and closed-source models, the study finds that even the best-performing model scores only 33.31 on average across the three dimensions, highlighting the significant challenge of step-level intent reasoning in egocentric video understanding.
为满足智能助手、机器人模仿学习和增强现实等应用对细粒度意图理解的需求，作者提出了EgoIntent，一个面向第一人称视频的步级意图理解基准。该数据集包含15个日常场景中的3014个步骤，每个片段在关键动作发生前截断，防止未来帧泄露，从而评估模型对“做什么”“为什么做”和“下一步做什么”的理解能力。在15个MLLM模型上测试发现，表现最好的模型平均得分仅为33.31，表明在第一人称视频中实现步级意图理解仍面临巨大挑战。
FlashMotion: Few-Step Controllable Video Generation with Trajectory Guidance
Authors: Quanhao Li, Zhen Xing, Rui Wang, Haidong Cao, Qi Dai, Daoguo Dong, Zuxuan Wu
First: 2026-03-12T16:45:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:45:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in trajectory-controllable video generation have achieved remarkable progress. Previous methods mainly use adapter-based architectures for precise motion control along predefined trajectories. However, all these methods rely on a multi-step denoising process, leading to substantial time redundancy and computational overhead. While existing video distillation methods successfully distill multi-step generators into few-step, directly applying these approaches to trajectory-controllable video generation results in noticeable degradation in both video quality and trajectory accuracy. To bridge this gap, we introduce FlashMotion, a novel training framework designed for few-step trajectory-controllable video generation. We first train a trajectory adapter on a multi-step video generator for precise trajectory control. Then, we distill the generator into a few-step version to accelerate video generation. Finally, we finetune the adapter using a hybrid strategy that combines diffusion and adversarial objectives, aligning it with the few-step generator to produce high-quality, trajectory-accurate videos. For evaluation, we introduce FlashBench, a benchmark for long-sequence trajectory-controllable video generation that measures both video quality and trajectory accuracy across varying numbers of foreground objects. Experiments on two adapter architectures show that FlashMotion surpasses existing video distillation methods and previous multi-step models in both visual quality and trajectory consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlashMotion：基于轨迹引导的少步可控视频生成
近期轨迹可控视频生成领域取得显著进展。此前方法主要采用适配器架构，沿预定义轨迹实现精确运动控制，但均依赖多步去噪过程，导致时间冗余和计算开销大。现有视频蒸馏方法虽成功将多步生成器蒸馏为少步版本，但直接应用于轨迹可控视频生成时，视频质量和轨迹精度均明显下降。为弥合此差距，我们提出FlashMotion，一种专为少步轨迹可控视频生成设计的新型训练框架。首先，我们在多步视频生成器上训练轨迹适配器以实现精确轨迹控制；接着，将生成器蒸馏为少步版本以加速视频生成；最后，采用结合扩散与对抗目标的混合策略微调适配器，使其与少步生成器对齐，生成高质量且轨迹精确的视频。为评估，我们引入FlashBench，一个衡量长序列轨迹可控视频生成中视频质量与轨迹精度的基准，支持不同前景物体数量。在两种适配器架构上的实验表明，FlashMotion在视觉质量和轨迹一致性上均优于现有蒸馏方法和先前的多步模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of multi-step trajectory-controllable video generation, FlashMotion introduces a novel training framework that distills multi-step video generators into few-step versions while preserving trajectory accuracy. It first trains a trajectory adapter on a multi-step generator, then distills the generator into a faster few-step model, and finally fine-tunes the adapter using a hybrid diffusion-adversarial strategy to align with the distilled generator. Experimental results on FlashBench demonstrate that FlashMotion outperforms existing distillation methods and prior multi-step models in both video quality and trajectory consistency across varying foreground object counts.
为解决多步轨迹可控视频生成效率低的问题，FlashMotion提出了一种新颖的训练框架，将多步视频生成器蒸馏为少步版本并保持轨迹精度。该方法首先在多步生成器上训练轨迹适配器，随后将其蒸馏为少步模型，最后采用混合扩散对抗策略微调适配器，使其与蒸馏后的生成器对齐。实验表明，FlashMotion在FlashBench新基准测试中，无论前景物体数量如何变化，均在视频质量和轨迹一致性上超越现有蒸馏方法和先前的多步模型。
Automatic Generation of High-Performance RL Environments
Authors: Seth Karten, Rahul Dev Appapogu, Chi Jin
First: 2026-03-12T16:45:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:45:47+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 9 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Translating complex reinforcement learning (RL) environments into high-performance implementations has traditionally required months of specialized engineering. We present a reusable recipe - a generic prompt template, hierarchical verification, and iterative agent-assisted repair - that produces semantically equivalent high-performance environments for <$10 in compute cost. We demonstrate three distinct workflows across five environments. Direct translation (no prior performance implementation exists): EmuRust (1.5x PPO speedup via Rust parallelism for a Game Boy emulator) and PokeJAX, the first GPU-parallel Pokemon battle simulator (500M SPS random action, 15.2M SPS PPO; 22,320x over the TypeScript reference). Translation verified against existing performance implementations: throughput parity with MJX (1.04x) and 5x over Brax at matched GPU batch sizes (HalfCheetah JAX); 42x PPO (Puffer Pong). New environment creation: TCGJax, the first deployable JAX Pokemon TCG engine (717K SPS random action, 153K SPS PPO; 6.6x over the Python reference), synthesized from a web-extracted specification. At 200M parameters, the environment overhead drops below 4% of training time. Hierarchical verification (property, interaction, and rollout tests) confirms semantic equivalence for all five environments; cross-backend policy transfer confirms zero sim-to-sim gap for all five environments. TCGJax, synthesized from a private reference absent from public repositories, serves as a contamination control for agent pretraining data concerns. The paper contains sufficient detail - including representative prompts, verification methodology, and complete results - that a coding agent could reproduce the translations directly from the manuscript.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高性能强化学习环境的自动生成
将复杂的强化学习（RL）环境转化为高性能实现传统上需要数月的专业工程。我们提出了一种可复用的配方——通用提示模板、分层验证和迭代式代理辅助修复——以低于10美元的计算成本生成语义等价的高性能环境。我们在五个环境中展示了三种不同工作流：直接翻译（无现有高性能实现）：EmuRust（通过Rust并行性实现Game Boy模拟器1.5倍PPO加速）和PokeJAX（首个GPU并行的宝可梦对战模拟器，随机动作5亿SPS，PPO 1520万SPS；相较TypeScript参考实现提升22320倍）；经现有高性能实现验证的翻译：与MJX吞吐量持平（1.04倍），在相同GPU批次大小下比Brax快5倍（HalfCheetah JAX）；Puffer Pong提升42倍PPO性能；新环境创建：TCGJax（首个可部署的JAX宝可梦TCG引擎，随机动作71.7万SPS，PPO 15.3万SPS；相较Python参考实现提升6.6倍），由网络提取的规范合成。在2亿参数规模下，环境开销低于训练时间的4%。分层验证（属性、交互、 rollout测试）确认五个环境语义等价；跨后端策略迁移验证五个环境间无模拟器间差距。TCGJax由私有参考实现合成（未公开于公共仓库），作为代理预训练数据污染控制。论文包含足够细节——包括代表性提示、验证方法和完整结果——使编码代理可直接从论文复现翻译。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high engineering cost of building high-performance reinforcement learning environments, the paper introduces a reusable recipe combining generic prompts, hierarchical verification, and agent-assisted iterative repair to automatically generate semantically equivalent, high-performance environments at low compute cost. Applied across five environments, the method achieves direct translation of emulators like EmuRust (1.5x PPO speedup) and PokeJAX (22,320x faster than TypeScript reference), matches or exceeds existing implementations (e.g., 5x faster than Brax on HalfCheetah), and synthesizes new environments like TCGJax (6.6x faster than Python reference) from web specifications. All environments pass hierarchical verification and show zero simulation-to-simulation performance gap across backends, with environment overhead under 4% at 200M parameters, and TCGJax serves as a contamination control for agent pretraining.
为降低构建高性能强化学习环境的工程成本，本文提出一套可复用的自动化方法，结合通用提示模板、分层验证与智能体辅助迭代修复，能在低成本下生成语义等价的高性能环境。在五个环境中应用该方法，实现了直接翻译（如EmuRust提速1.5倍、PokeJAX比TypeScript参考实现快22320倍）、匹配或超越现有实现（如HalfCheetah上比Brax快5倍）、以及从网页规范合成新环境（如TCGJax比Python参考实现快6.6倍）。所有环境均通过分层验证，跨后端策略迁移无性能差异，环境开销在2亿参数下低于训练时间的4%，且TCGJax作为私有参考环境，可用于验证智能体预训练数据的污染控制。
Estimating Canopy Height at Scale
Authors: Jan Pauls, Max Zimmer, Una M. Kelly, Martin Schwartz, Sassan Saatchi, Philippe Ciais, Sebastian Pokutta, Martin Brandt, Fabian Gieseke
Venue: ICML
First: 2024-06-03T07:53:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:45:44+00:00
Comments: ICML Camera-Ready, 17 pages, 14 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a framework for global-scale canopy height estimation based on satellite data. Our model leverages advanced data preprocessing techniques, resorts to a novel loss function designed to counter geolocation inaccuracies inherent in the ground-truth height measurements, and employs data from the Shuttle Radar Topography Mission to effectively filter out erroneous labels in mountainous regions, enhancing the reliability of our predictions in those areas. A comparison between predictions and ground-truth labels yields an MAE / RMSE of 2.43 / 4.73 (meters) overall and 4.45 / 6.72 (meters) for trees taller than five meters, which depicts a substantial improvement compared to existing global-scale maps. The resulting height map as well as the underlying framework will facilitate and enhance ecological analyses at a global scale, including, but not limited to, large-scale forest and biomass monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大规模冠层高度估算
我们提出了一种基于卫星数据的全球尺度冠层高度估算框架。该模型采用先进的数据预处理技术，引入一种新型损失函数以应对地面真值高度测量中固有的地理位置误差，并利用航天飞机雷达地形任务数据有效过滤山区的错误标签，从而提升这些区域预测结果的可靠性。预测值与地面真值标签对比显示，整体MAE/RMSE为2.43/4.73米，树高超过五米的区域为4.45/6.72米，相较现有全球尺度地图有显著改进。所得高度图及底层框架将有助于并提升全球范围内的生态分析，包括但不限于大规模森林与生物量监测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate global canopy height data for ecological monitoring, this study introduces a satellite-based framework that preprocesses data, uses a custom loss function to mitigate geolocation errors in ground-truth labels, and leverages Shuttle Radar Topography Mission data to filter noisy labels in mountainous regions. Experimental results show an overall MAE/RMSE of 2.43/4.73 meters and 4.45/6.72 meters for trees over five meters tall, significantly outperforming existing global maps and enabling more reliable large-scale forest and biomass analysis.
为支持全球生态监测，该研究提出一种基于卫星数据的树冠高度估算框架，通过先进数据预处理、定制损失函数缓解地面真值定位误差，并利用航天器雷达地形任务数据过滤山地区域错误标签。实验结果显示，整体MAE/RMSE为2.43/4.73米，高于5米树木为4.45/6.72米，显著优于现有全球地图，可有效提升大范围森林与生物量监测的准确性。
O3N: Omnidirectional Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction
Authors: Mengfei Duan, Hao Shi, Fei Teng, Guoqiang Zhao, Yuheng Zhang, Zhiyong Li, Kailun Yang
First: 2026-03-12T16:45:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:45:42+00:00
Comments: The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/MengfeiD/O3N
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding and reconstructing the 3D world through omnidirectional perception is an inevitable trend in the development of autonomous agents and embodied intelligence. However, existing 3D occupancy prediction methods are constrained by limited perspective inputs and predefined training distribution, making them difficult to apply to embodied agents that require comprehensive and safe perception of scenes in open world exploration. To address this, we present O3N, the first purely visual, end-to-end Omnidirectional Open-vocabulary Occupancy predictioN framework. O3N embeds omnidirectional voxels in a polar-spiral topology via the Polar-spiral Mamba (PsM) module, enabling continuous spatial representation and long-range context modeling across 360°. The Occupancy Cost Aggregation (OCA) module introduces a principled mechanism for unifying geometric and semantic supervision within the voxel space, ensuring consistency between the reconstructed geometry and the underlying semantic structure. Moreover, Natural Modality Alignment (NMA) establishes a gradient-free alignment pathway that harmonizes visual features, voxel embeddings, and text semantics, forming a consistent "pixel-voxel-text" representation triad. Extensive experiments on multiple models demonstrate that our method not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on QuadOcc and Human360Occ benchmarks but also exhibits remarkable cross-scene generalization and semantic scalability, paving the way toward universal 3D world modeling. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/MengfeiD/O3N.
中文标题/摘要
标题：O3N：全方位开放词汇占用预测
通过全方位感知理解并重建三维世界，是自主代理与具身智能发展的必然趋势。然而，现有三维占用预测方法受限于有限视角输入和预定义训练分布，难以应用于需要在开放世界探索中全面、安全感知场景的具身代理。为此，我们提出O3N，首个纯视觉、端到端的全方位开放词汇占用预测框架。O3N通过极螺旋Mamba（PsM）模块，将全方位体素嵌入极螺旋拓扑结构，实现连续空间表示与360°长程上下文建模。占用成本聚合（OCA）模块在体素空间内引入几何与语义监督统一的机制，确保重建几何结构与底层语义结构的一致性。此外，自然模态对齐（NMA）建立无梯度对齐路径，协调视觉特征、体素嵌入与文本语义，形成一致的“像素-体素-文本”三元表示。在多个模型上的大量实验表明，我们的方法不仅在QuadOcc和Human360Occ基准上达到最先进性能，还展现出卓越的跨场景泛化能力与语义可扩展性，为通用三维世界建模奠定基础。源代码将在https://github.com/MengfeiD/O3N公开发布。
Summary / 总结
To enable autonomous agents to perceive and reconstruct 3D environments from omnidirectional visual inputs without relying on predefined vocabularies or limited viewpoints, the authors introduce O3N, a purely visual, end-to-end framework for open-vocabulary occupancy prediction. O3N uses a Polar-spiral Mamba module to encode 360-degree voxel data in a continuous polar-spiral topology, supporting long-range spatial modeling. It further integrates geometric and semantic supervision via Occupancy Cost Aggregation and aligns visual, voxel, and text representations through Natural Modality Alignment. Experiments on QuadOcc and Human360Occ show O3N achieves state-of-the-art results and demonstrates strong cross-scene generalization and semantic scalability, advancing universal 3D world modeling.
为使自主智能体能从全景视觉输入中无预定义词汇地感知并重建3D环境，作者提出O3N框架，这是一个纯视觉、端到端的开放词汇占用预测方法。该框架通过极螺旋Mamba模块将360度体素数据编码为连续的极螺旋拓扑结构，支持长距离空间建模；并通过占用成本聚合统一几何与语义监督，借助自然模态对齐建立视觉、体素与文本表示间的无梯度对齐路径。在QuadOcc和Human360Occ数据集上的实验表明，O3N达到当前最优性能，并展现出优异的跨场景泛化能力和语义可扩展性，推动通用3D世界建模的发展。
HATS: Hardness-Aware Trajectory Synthesis for GUI Agents
Authors: Rui Shao, Ruize Gao, Bin Xie, Yixing Li, Kaiwen Zhou, Shuai Wang, Weili Guan, Gongwei Chen
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T16:40:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:40:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graphical user interface (GUI) agents powered by large vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable potential in automating digital tasks, highlighting the need for high-quality trajectory data to support effective agent training. Yet existing trajectory synthesis pipelines often yield agents that fail to generalize beyond simple interactions. We identify this limitation as stemming from the neglect of semantically ambiguous actions, whose meanings are context-dependent, sequentially dependent, or visually ambiguous. Such actions are crucial for real-world robustness but are under-represented and poorly processed in current datasets, leading to semantic misalignment between task instructions and execution. To address these issues, we propose HATS, a Hardness-Aware Trajectory Synthesis framework designed to mitigate the impact of semantic ambiguity. We define hardness as the degree of semantic ambiguity associated with an action and develop two complementary modules: (1) hardness-driven exploration, which guides data collection toward ambiguous yet informative interactions, and (2) alignment-guided refinement, which iteratively validates and repairs instruction-execution alignment. The two modules operate in a closed loop: exploration supplies refinement with challenging trajectories, while refinement feedback updates the hardness signal to guide future exploration. Extensive experiments show that agents trained with HATS consistently outperform state-of-the-art baselines across benchmark GUI environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HATS：面向GUI代理的语义硬度感知轨迹合成
基于大型视觉-语言模型（VLM）的图形用户界面（GUI）代理在自动化数字任务方面展现出巨大潜力，凸显了高质量轨迹数据对有效代理训练的重要性。然而，现有轨迹合成流程常导致代理无法泛化至复杂交互。我们识别出该局限源于对语义模糊动作的忽视，此类动作含义依赖上下文、序列或视觉模糊。这些动作对现实世界鲁棒性至关重要，但在当前数据集中代表性不足且处理不佳，导致任务指令与执行之间语义错位。为解决这些问题，我们提出HATS框架，一种语义硬度感知的轨迹合成方法，旨在减轻语义模糊的影响。我们定义“硬度”为动作语义模糊的程度，并开发两个互补模块：（1）硬度驱动探索，引导数据采集聚焦于模糊但信息丰富的交互；（2）对齐引导优化，迭代验证并修复指令-执行对齐。两个模块形成闭环：探索为优化提供挑战性轨迹，优化反馈则更新硬度信号以指导后续探索。大量实验表明，使用HATS训练的代理在基准GUI环境中持续优于现有最先进基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of GUI agents trained on existing trajectory data, this work identifies semantic ambiguity in actions as a key bottleneck. HATS introduces a hardness-aware framework that defines action ambiguity as a signal to guide data collection and refinement. It combines hardness-driven exploration to sample challenging, context-dependent interactions and alignment-guided refinement to iteratively correct instruction-execution mismatches, operating in a closed loop. Experiments demonstrate that HATS-trained agents significantly outperform prior methods across multiple GUI benchmarks, achieving higher success rates and robustness in complex, ambiguous scenarios.
为了解决现有GUI代理在训练数据中缺乏语义模糊动作导致泛化能力差的问题，本文提出HATS框架，通过定义动作的语义模糊程度为“难度”，构建两个互补模块：难度驱动探索模块用于采集具有挑战性但信息丰富的交互，以及对齐引导优化模块用于迭代修正指令与执行之间的偏差。两个模块形成闭环：优化模块的反馈用于更新难度信号以指导后续探索。实验表明，使用HATS训练的代理在多个GUI基准测试中持续优于当前最先进方法。
Multi-lingual Functional Evaluation for Large Language Models
Authors: Victor Ojewale, Inioluwa Deborah Raji, Suresh Venkatasubramanian
First: 2025-06-25T19:32:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:38:30+00:00
Comments: This is an updated version with details of the CL-GSM Symbolic and CL-IFEval datasets validation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-lingual competence in large language models is often evaluated via static data benchmarks such as Belebele, M-MMLU and M-GSM. However, these evaluations often fail to provide an adequate understanding of the practical performance and robustness of models across multi-lingual settings. In response, we create multi-lingual functional benchmarks -- Cross-Lingual Grade School Math Symbolic (CL-GSM Symbolic) and Cross-Lingual Instruction-Following Eval (CL-IFEval)-- by translating existing functional benchmark templates from English to five additional languages that span the range of resources available for NLP: French, Spanish, Hindi, Arabic and Yoruba. Our results reveal that some static multi-lingual benchmarks capture functional performance much more closely than others (i.e. across models, there is a 24%, 17% and 18% decrease in performance between M-GSM and CL-GSM Symbolic in English, French and Spanish respectively; similarly there's a 15 - 24% performance drop across languages between Belebele and CL-IFEval, and only a 0.5% to 3% performance drop between M-MMLU and CL-IFEval). Similarly, we find that model robustness across languages varies significantly, with certain languages (eg. Arabic, English) being the most consistently well performing across evaluation iterations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型多语言功能评估
大语言模型的多语言能力常通过静态数据基准（如Belebele、M-MMLU和M-GSM）进行评估。然而，这些评估往往无法充分反映模型在多语言环境中的实际表现与鲁棒性。为此，我们创建了两个多语言功能基准——跨语言小学数学符号基准（CL-GSM Symbolic）和跨语言指令遵循评估（CL-IFEval），通过将现有功能基准模板从英语翻译至五种涵盖NLP资源范围的语言：法语、西班牙语、印地语、阿拉伯语和约鲁巴语。结果表明，部分静态多语言基准比其他基准更贴近功能表现（例如，在英语、法语和西班牙语中，M-GSM与CL-GSM Symbolic的性能分别下降24%、17%和18%；同样，Belebele与CL-IFEval在各语言间的性能下降为15%-24%，而M-MMLU与CL-IFEval仅下降0.5%-3%）。此外，我们发现模型在不同语言间的鲁棒性差异显著，某些语言（如阿拉伯语、英语）在多次评估中表现最为稳定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static multi-lingual benchmarks in capturing real-world model performance, the authors introduce two new functional benchmarks—CL-GSM Symbolic and CL-IFEval—translated into five non-English languages (French, Spanish, Hindi, Arabic, Yoruba) to assess cross-lingual symbolic reasoning and instruction-following. Experimental results show that CL-GSM Symbolic outperforms static benchmarks like M-GSM by 24% in English, 17% in French, and 18% in Spanish, while CL-IFEval shows a 15–24% performance gap over Belebele and only a 0.5–3% gap over M-MMLU. The study also reveals that model robustness varies significantly across languages, with Arabic and English consistently performing best across evaluation iterations.
为弥补静态多语言基准在评估模型实际表现和鲁棒性方面的不足，作者构建了两个新的功能型基准——CL-GSM Symbolic 和 CL-IFEval，并将其翻译成五种非英语语言（法语、西班牙语、印地语、阿拉伯语、约鲁巴语），用于评估跨语言符号推理和指令遵循能力。实验结果表明，CL-GSM Symbolic 在英语、法语和西班牙语中分别比 M-GSM 高出 24%、17% 和 18%；CL-IFEval 与 Belebele 相比性能下降 15–24%，与 M-MMLU 相比仅下降 0.5–3%。此外，模型在不同语言间的鲁棒性差异显著，阿拉伯语和英语在多次评估中表现最为稳定。
TopoBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Hard Topological Reasoning
Authors: Mayug Maniparambil, Nils Hoehing, Janak Kapuriya, Arjun Karuvally, Ellen Rushe, Anthony Ventresque, Noel O'Connor, Fergal Reid
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T16:37:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:37:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted, Workshop on Logical Reasoning of Large Language Models at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Solving topological grid puzzles requires reasoning over global spatial invariants such as connectivity, loop closure, and region symmetry and remains challenging for even the most powerful large language models (LLMs). To study these abilities under controlled settings, we introduce TopoBench, a benchmark of six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. We evaluate strong reasoning LLMs on TopoBench and find that even frontier models solve fewer than one quarter of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolved. To investigate whether these failures stem from reasoning limitations or from difficulty extracting and maintaining spatial constraints, we annotate 750 chain of thought traces with an error taxonomy that surfaces four candidate causal failure modes, then test them with targeted interventions simulating each error type. These interventions show that certain error patterns like premature commitment and constraint forgetting have a direct impact on the ability to solve the puzzle, while repeated reasoning is a benign effect of search. Finally we study mitigation strategies including prompt guidance, cell-aligned grid representations and tool-based constraint checking, finding that the bottleneck lies in extracting constraints from spatial representations and not in reasoning over them. Code and data are available at github.com/mayug/topobench-benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TopoBench：在复杂拓扑推理上评估大语言模型
解决拓扑网格谜题需要对全局空间不变量（如连通性、环闭合和区域对称性）进行推理，即使是最强大的大语言模型（LLMs）也面临挑战。为在可控环境下研究这些能力，我们引入TopoBench，包含六个谜题家族、三个难度等级的基准测试。我们在TopoBench上评估强推理LLMs，发现即使是前沿模型也仅能解决不到四分之一的难题实例，其中两个家族几乎未被解决。为探究这些失败源于推理能力限制还是空间约束提取与维护困难，我们对750条思维链进行标注，构建错误分类体系，识别出四种潜在因果失效模式，并通过针对性干预模拟每种错误类型进行测试。结果表明，某些错误模式（如过早承诺和约束遗忘）直接影响解题能力，而重复推理是搜索过程的良性副产物。最后，我们研究了缓解策略，包括提示引导、单元对齐网格表示和基于工具的约束检查，发现瓶颈在于从空间表示中提取约束，而非推理过程本身。代码和数据可在github.com/mayug/topobench-benchmark获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty LLMs face in solving topological grid puzzles that require global spatial reasoning, the authors introduce TopoBench, a benchmark with six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. They evaluate frontier LLMs and find that even the best models solve fewer than 25% of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolvable. To diagnose failure causes, they annotate 750 chain-of-thought traces with an error taxonomy, identifying four failure modes, and test interventions simulating each. Results show that premature commitment and constraint forgetting significantly impair performance, while repeated reasoning is harmless. Mitigation strategies like prompt guidance and grid representations improve results, but the core bottleneck remains extracting spatial constraints, not reasoning over them.
为研究大语言模型在需要全局空间推理的拓扑网格谜题上的能力，作者提出了TopoBench基准，包含六个谜题家族和三个难度等级。实验发现，即使是最先进的模型在难题上正确率也低于25%，其中两个家族几乎无法解决。通过标注750条思维链并建立错误分类体系，作者识别出四种失败模式，并通过针对性干预验证其影响，结果表明“过早承诺”和“约束遗忘”显著降低解题能力，而重复推理无害。缓解策略如提示引导和网格对齐表示虽有帮助，但核心瓶颈仍在于从空间表示中提取约束，而非推理过程本身。
Lifelong Imitation Learning with Multimodal Latent Replay and Incremental Adjustment
Authors: Fanqi Yu, Matteo Tiezzi, Tommaso Apicella, Cigdem Beyan, Vittorio Murino
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-11T16:13:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:36:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce a lifelong imitation learning framework that enables continual policy refinement across sequential tasks under realistic memory and data constraints. Our approach departs from conventional experience replay by operating entirely in a multimodal latent space, where compact representations of visual, linguistic, and robot's state information are stored and reused to support future learning. To further stabilize adaptation, we introduce an incremental feature adjustment mechanism that regularizes the evolution of task embeddings through an angular margin constraint, preserving inter-task distinctiveness. Our method establishes a new state of the art in the LIBERO benchmarks, achieving 10-17 point gains in AUC and up to 65% less forgetting compared to previous leading methods. Ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of each component, showing consistent gains over alternative strategies. The code is available at: https://github.com/yfqi/lifelong_mlr_ifa.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态潜在重放与增量调整的终身模仿学习
我们提出了一种终身模仿学习框架，可在现实的记忆和数据约束下，持续优化跨序列任务的策略。该方法不同于传统经验回放，完全在多模态潜在空间中运作，其中存储并重用视觉、语言和机器人状态信息的紧凑表示以支持未来学习。为进一步稳定适应，我们引入了增量特征调整机制，通过角度间隔约束正则化任务嵌入的演化，保持任务间差异性。该方法在LIBERO基准测试中达到新状态，AUC提升10-17分，遗忘率比先前领先方法降低65%。消融研究证实各组件的有效性，显示相较替代策略持续收益。代码见：https://github.com/yfqi/lifelong_mlr_ifa。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for continual policy improvement in lifelong imitation learning under memory and data constraints, this work proposes a framework that operates in a multimodal latent space, storing compact representations of visual, linguistic, and robotic state data for reuse. To stabilize adaptation across tasks, it introduces an incremental feature adjustment mechanism that enforces angular margin constraints on task embeddings to preserve distinctiveness. Experiments on LIBERO benchmarks show the method achieves 10-17 point gains in AUC and up to 65% less forgetting compared to prior state-of-the-art approaches, with ablation studies confirming the individual contributions of each component.
为了解决在记忆与数据受限条件下持续改进策略的需求，该研究提出一种在多模态潜在空间中操作的框架，存储视觉、语言和机器人状态的紧凑表示以供重用。为稳定跨任务适应，引入增量特征调整机制，通过角度边际约束调节任务嵌入的演化，保持任务间差异性。在LIBERO基准测试中，该方法相比先前最先进方法在AUC上提升10-17分，遗忘率降低高达65%，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。
On the Theoretical Limitations of Embedding-Based Retrieval
Authors: Orion Weller, Michael Boratko, Iftekhar Naim, Jinhyuk Lee
Venue: ICLR
First: 2025-08-28T17:43:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:35:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vector embeddings have been tasked with an ever-increasing set of retrieval tasks over the years, with a nascent rise in using them for reasoning, instruction-following, coding, and more. These new benchmarks push embeddings to work for any query and any notion of relevance that could be given. While prior works have pointed out theoretical limitations of vector embeddings, there is a common assumption that these difficulties are exclusively due to unrealistic queries, and those that are not can be overcome with better training data and larger models. In this work, we demonstrate that we may encounter these theoretical limitations in realistic settings with extremely simple queries. We connect known results in learning theory, showing that the number of top-k subsets of documents capable of being returned as the result of some query is limited by the dimension of the embedding. We empirically show that this holds true even if we directly optimize on the test set with free parameterized embeddings. Using free embeddings, we then demonstrate that returning all pairs of documents requires a relatively high dimension. We then create a realistic dataset called LIMIT that stress tests embedding models based on these theoretical results, and observe that even state-of-the-art models fail on this dataset despite the simple nature of the task. Our work shows the limits of embedding models under the existing single vector paradigm and calls for future research to develop new techniques that can resolve this fundamental limitation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于嵌入的检索的理论局限性研究
近年来，向量嵌入被赋予了越来越多的检索任务，包括推理、指令遵循、编程等新兴应用。这些新基准要求嵌入能够应对任何查询和任何相关性定义。尽管已有研究指出了向量嵌入的理论局限性，但普遍认为这些困难仅源于不现实的查询，而现实中的查询可通过更好的训练数据和更大的模型克服。本文证明，即使在现实场景中使用极其简单的查询，也可能遭遇这些理论局限。我们结合学习理论中的已知结果，表明可被某些查询返回的前k个文档子集数量受限于嵌入的维度。我们通过在测试集上直接优化参数化嵌入，实证验证了这一结论。进一步地，我们使用自由嵌入证明，返回所有文档对需要较高的维度。随后，我们构建了一个名为LIMIT的现实数据集，基于上述理论结果对嵌入模型进行压力测试，发现即使是最先进的模型在该数据集上也失败，尽管任务本身极为简单。本研究揭示了现有单向量范式下嵌入模型的局限性，并呼吁未来研究开发能解决这一根本限制的新技术。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the theoretical constraints of embedding-based retrieval systems, challenging the assumption that limitations arise only from unrealistic queries. It connects learning theory to show that the number of possible top-k document subsets retrievable via embeddings is bounded by embedding dimension, regardless of training data or model size. Empirical experiments using free-parameterized embeddings confirm this bound, demonstrating that even simple tasks like returning all document pairs require high dimensions. The authors introduce the LIMIT dataset, which exploits these theoretical limits, and show that state-of-the-art models fail on it despite its simplicity, indicating fundamental limitations of the single-vector paradigm.
本文探讨了基于嵌入的检索系统的理论限制，挑战了‘限制仅源于不现实查询’的假设。通过连接学习理论，作者证明无论训练数据或模型规模如何，可被嵌入检索出的前k个文档子集数量受嵌入维度限制。实验表明，即使使用自由参数化嵌入直接优化测试集，该限制依然成立，且返回所有文档对需高维嵌入。作者构建了名为LIMIT的现实数据集，利用这些理论限制对模型进行压力测试，发现即使任务简单，当前最先进模型仍无法通过，揭示了单向量范式的基本局限性。
Increasing intelligence in AI agents can worsen collective outcomes
Authors: Neil F. Johnson
First: 2026-03-12T16:31:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:31:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When resources are scarce, will a population of AI agents coordinate in harmony, or descend into tribal chaos? Diverse decision-making AI from different developers is entering everyday devices -- from phones and medical devices to battlefield drones and cars -- and these AI agents typically compete for finite shared resources such as charging slots, relay bandwidth, and traffic priority. Yet their collective dynamics and hence risks to users and society are poorly understood. Here we study AI-agent populations as the first system of real agents in which four key variables governing collective behaviour can be independently toggled: nature (innate LLM diversity), nurture (individual reinforcement learning), culture (emergent tribe formation), and resource scarcity. We show empirically and mathematically that when resources are scarce, AI model diversity and reinforcement learning increase dangerous system overload, though tribe formation lessens this risk. Meanwhile, some individuals profit handsomely. When resources are abundant, the same ingredients drive overload to near zero, though tribe formation makes the overload slightly worse. The crossover is arithmetical: it is where opposing tribes that form spontaneously first fit inside the available capacity. More sophisticated AI-agent populations are not better: whether their sophistication helps or harms depends entirely on a single number -- the capacity-to-population ratio -- that is knowable before any AI-agent ships.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能代理的智能提升可能恶化集体结果
当资源稀缺时，人工智能代理群体是和谐协作还是陷入部落式混乱？来自不同开发者的多样化决策AI正进入日常设备——从手机和医疗设备到战场无人机和汽车——这些AI代理通常竞争有限的共享资源，如充电插槽、中继带宽和交通优先权。然而，它们的集体动态及其对用户和社会的风险尚不明确。本文首次将AI代理群体作为可独立调节四大关键变量的系统进行研究：本性（固有LLM多样性）、养育（个体强化学习）、文化（自发部落形成）和资源稀缺性。我们通过实证和数学方法表明，当资源稀缺时，AI模型多样性与强化学习会加剧系统过载风险，而部落形成可降低此风险；同时部分个体获利颇丰。当资源充足时，相同因素使过载接近零，但部落形成会使过载略有恶化。转折点是算术性的：即自发形成的对立部落首次能容纳于可用容量内时。更复杂的AI代理群体并不更好：其复杂性是帮助还是伤害，完全取决于一个可预先知晓的单一数值——容量与人口比例。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how diverse AI agents interact under resource constraints, this study examines collective behavior in populations of AI agents by independently varying four key factors: innate model diversity, individual reinforcement learning, emergent tribe formation, and resource scarcity. Through empirical and mathematical analysis, it finds that under scarce resources, increased AI sophistication and diversity lead to dangerous system overload, while tribe formation mitigates this risk, even as some agents benefit disproportionately. In abundant resource conditions, the same factors suppress overload nearly to zero, though tribe formation slightly worsens it. The critical tipping point is determined by the capacity-to-population ratio — a single, pre-deployment measurable — which dictates whether increased intelligence improves or harms collective outcomes.
鉴于日益普及的AI代理在资源受限环境中部署，本研究探讨了智能水平与集体行为在稀缺条件下的相互作用。通过可独立调节四个变量——模型多样性、个体强化学习、部落形成和资源稀缺性——的控制实验，作者发现，在资源稀缺时，AI智能提升和强化学习会加剧系统过载，而部落形成可缓解此风险；资源充足时，过载几乎消失，但部落形成略有恶化。关键在于一个单一指标：容量与代理数量之比，它决定了更智能的代理群体是改善还是恶化集体结果。
Hoi3DGen: Generating High-Quality Human-Object-Interactions in 3D
Authors: Agniv Sharma, Xianghui Xie, Tom Fischer, Eddy Ilg, Gerard Pons-Moll
First: 2026-03-12T16:27:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:27:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling and generating 3D human-object interactions from text is crucial for applications in AR, XR, and gaming. Existing approaches often rely on score distillation from text-to-image models, but their results suffer from the Janus problem and do not follow text prompts faithfully due to the scarcity of high-quality interaction data. We introduce Hoi3DGen, a framework that generates high-quality textured meshes of human-object interaction that follow the input interaction descriptions precisely. We first curate realistic and high-quality interaction data leveraging multimodal large language models, and then create a full text-to-3D pipeline, which achieves orders-of-magnitude improvements in interaction fidelity. Our method surpasses baselines by 4-15x in text consistency and 3-7x in 3D model quality, exhibiting strong generalization to diverse categories and interaction types, while maintaining high-quality 3D generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hoi3DGen：生成高质量的人机交互3D模型
从文本建模和生成3D人机交互对对于AR、XR和游戏应用至关重要。现有方法通常依赖于从文本到图像模型中蒸馏得分，但其结果因高质量交互数据稀缺而面临雅努斯问题，无法忠实遵循文本提示。我们提出了Hoi3DGen框架，可精确生成符合输入交互描述的高质量纹理化3D人机交互模型。我们首先利用多模态大语言模型整理真实且高质量的交互数据，然后构建完整的文本到3D生成管道，显著提升了交互保真度。我们的方法在文本一致性上比基线提升4-15倍，在3D模型质量上提升3-7倍，展现出对多样类别和交互类型的强泛化能力，同时保持高质量3D生成。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate 3D human-object interaction generation for AR, XR, and gaming, Hoi3DGen addresses the limitations of existing text-to-image-based methods that suffer from poor fidelity and prompt misalignment due to scarce high-quality data. The method first constructs a curated dataset of realistic interactions using multimodal large language models, then builds a full text-to-3D pipeline that directly generates textured 3D meshes. Experimental results show Hoi3DGen outperforms baselines by 4-15x in text consistency and 3-7x in 3D model quality, with strong generalization across diverse interaction types and categories.
为满足AR、XR和游戏领域对高质量3D人机交互生成的需求，Hoi3DGen针对现有基于文本到图像方法因数据稀缺导致的保真度低和提示不匹配问题，提出了一种新框架。该方法首先利用多模态大语言模型构建真实且高质量的交互数据集，再构建完整的文本到3D生成流水线，直接输出纹理化的3D网格。实验表明，该方法在文本一致性上比基线高4-15倍，在3D模型质量上高3-7倍，且在多种交互类型和类别上具备良好泛化能力。
WideSeek-R1: Exploring Width Scaling for Broad Information Seeking via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Zelai Xu, Zhexuan Xu, Ruize Zhang, Chunyang Zhu, Shi Yu, Weilin Liu, Quanlu Zhang, Wenbo Ding, Chao Yu, Yu Wang
First: 2026-02-04T15:05:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:26:55+00:00
Comments: https://wideseek-r1.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have largely focused on depth scaling, where a single agent solves long-horizon problems with multi-turn reasoning and tool use. However, as tasks grow broader, the key bottleneck shifts from individual competence to organizational capability. In this work, we explore a complementary dimension of width scaling with multi-agent systems to address broad information seeking. Existing multi-agent systems often rely on hand-crafted workflows and turn-taking interactions that fail to parallelize work effectively. To bridge this gap, we propose WideSeek-R1, a lead-agent-subagent framework trained via multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) to synergize scalable orchestration and parallel execution. By utilizing a shared LLM with isolated contexts and specialized tools, WideSeek-R1 jointly optimizes the lead agent and parallel subagents on a curated dataset of 20k broad information-seeking tasks. Extensive experiments show that WideSeek-R1-4B achieves an item F1 score of 40.0% on the WideSearch benchmark, which is comparable to the performance of single-agent DeepSeek-R1-671B. Furthermore, WideSeek-R1-4B exhibits consistent performance gains as the number of parallel subagents increases, highlighting the effectiveness of width scaling.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of depth scaling in LLMs for broad information seeking, this work introduces WideSeek-R1, a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that scales via width rather than depth. It employs a lead-agent-subagent architecture with shared LLMs and isolated contexts to enable parallel task execution and coordinated orchestration. Trained on 20k broad information-seeking tasks, the system jointly optimizes agent roles and tool usage. Experimental results show that WideSeek-R1-4B achieves 40.0% F1 on the WideSearch benchmark, matching the performance of a 671B single-agent model, and demonstrates consistent performance gains with more parallel subagents.
为应对大语言模型在广域信息检索任务中深度扩展的局限，本研究提出WideSeek-R1框架，通过宽度扩展而非深度扩展提升多智能体协作能力。该框架采用主智能体-子智能体架构，共享LLM并隔离上下文，实现任务并行与协同调度。在2万条广域信息检索任务数据集上联合优化智能体角色与工具使用。实验表明，WideSeek-R1-4B在WideSearch基准上F1分数达40.0%，与671B单智能体模型表现相当，且随并行子智能体数量增加持续提升性能。
Cross-Context Review: Improving LLM Output Quality by Separating Production and Review Sessions
Authors: Tae-Eun Song
First: 2026-03-12T16:26:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:26:38+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models struggle to catch errors in their own outputs when the review happens in the same session that produced them. This paper introduces Cross-Context Review (CCR), a straightforward method where the review is conducted in a fresh session with no access to the production conversation history. We ran a controlled experiment: 30 artifacts (code, technical documents, presentation scripts) with 150 injected errors, tested under four review conditions -- same-session Self-Review (SR), repeated Self-Review (SR2), context-aware Subagent Review (SA), and Cross-Context Review (CCR). Over 360 reviews, CCR reached an F1 of 28.6%, outperforming SR (24.6%, p=0.008, d=0.52), SR2 (21.7%, p<0.001, d=0.72), and SA (23.8%, p=0.004, d=0.57). The SR2 result matters most for interpretation: reviewing twice in the same session did not beat reviewing once (p=0.11), which rules out repetition as an explanation for CCR's advantage. The benefit comes from context separation itself. CCR works with any model, needs no infrastructure, and costs only one extra session.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨上下文审查：通过分离生成与审查会话提升大语言模型输出质量
大语言模型在自身输出的同一会话中进行审查时难以发现错误。本文提出跨上下文审查（CCR）方法，即在无生成对话历史的新会话中进行审查。我们进行了受控实验：30个（代码、技术文档、演示脚本）人工注入150个错误，测试四种审查方式——同会话自审查（SR）、重复自审查（SR2）、上下文感知子代理审查（SA）和跨上下文审查（CCR）。在360次审查中，CCR达到F1值28.6%，显著优于SR（24.6%，p=0.008，d=0.52）、SR2（21.7%，p<0.001，d=0.72）和SA（23.8%，p=0.004，d=0.57）。SR2结果对解释至关重要：同会话重复审查并未优于单次审查（p=0.11），排除重复作为CCR优势的解释。优势源于上下文分离本身。CCR适用于任何模型，无需基础设施，仅需额外一个会话。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation of large language models in self-correcting their outputs when review occurs within the same session as production. The authors propose Cross-Context Review (CCR), a method that isolates review into a separate session with no access to prior conversation history. In a controlled experiment involving 30 artifacts with 150 injected errors, CCR achieved an F1 score of 28.6%, significantly outperforming same-session self-review (24.6%, p=0.008), repeated self-review (21.7%, p<0.001), and subagent review (23.8%, p=0.004). Crucially, the lack of improvement from repeated self-review suggests that context separation—not repetition—is the key driver of CCR’s effectiveness, and the method requires no special infrastructure or cost beyond one additional session.
该论文指出，大语言模型在同一次会话中进行自我审查时难以发现自身输出的错误。为此，作者提出跨上下文审查（CCR）方法，将生成与审查分隔在不同会话中，确保审查阶段无法访问生成过程的历史对话。在包含30个带150处注入错误的工件的对照实验中，CCR以28.6%的F1分数显著优于同会话自我审查（24.6%）、重复自我审查（21.7%）和上下文感知子代理审查（23.8%）。特别值得注意的是，重复审查并未提升效果，说明CCR的优势源于上下文分离而非重复机制。该方法无需特殊基础设施，兼容任何模型，仅需额外一次会话。
From Toil to Thought: Designing for Strategic Exploration and Responsible AI in Systematic Literature Reviews
Authors: Runlong Ye, Naaz Sibia, Angela Zavaleta Bernuy, Tingting Zhu, Carolina Nobre, Viktoria Pammer-Schindler, Michael Liut
First: 2026-01-21T00:28:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:25:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IUI 26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Systematic Literature Reviews (SLRs) are fundamental to scientific progress, yet the process is hindered by a fragmented tool ecosystem that imposes a high cognitive load. This friction suppresses the iterative, exploratory nature of scholarly work. To investigate these challenges, we conducted an exploratory design study with 20 experienced researchers. This study identified key friction points: 1) the high cognitive load of managing iterative query refinement across multiple databases, 2) the overwhelming scale and pace of publication of modern literature, and 3) the tension between automation and scholarly agency. Informed by these findings, we developed ARC, a design probe that operationalizes solutions for multi-database integration, transparent iterative search, and verifiable AI-assisted screening. A comparative user study with 8 researchers suggests that an integrated environment facilitates a transition in scholarly work, moving researchers from managing administrative overhead to engaging in strategic exploration. By utilizing external representations to scaffold strategic exploration and transparent AI reasoning, our system supports verifiable judgment, aiming to augment expert contributions from initial creation through long-term maintenance of knowledge synthesis.
中文标题/摘要
